## High (45)


### src/main/ipc/ipc-server.ts

| id | Electron | Status | Area | Behaviour | Detail | Nearest Rust |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| [c08-81](chunks/c08-main_ipc_ipc-server.md) | src/main/ipc/ipc-server.ts:748 | PARTIAL | Session sync | connectionReconnected: before rebuilding the session after a reconnect, the renderer flushes the old store's deferred or failed workspace body writes on the new transport (src/renderer/store/app.ts:390-419) | The rebuild is ported: each Connected generation gets a fresh SessionStore. The flush is not. AppStore disposes the old store as soon as the status leaves Connected, which clears its syncs. A body write that failed or was in flight at the drop is marked dirty ('re-written by the next reconnect flush') and then thrown away, and the new store rebuilds from disk without it. flush_deferred_writes and set_connection_status exist, but nothing in app-rs calls them, tests included. Commit 756cd59 lists this as 'Not yet'. It is not among the README's deliberate differences. | app-rs/crates/treeterm-model/src/app_store.rs:235 (retire on every status change); app-rs/crates/treeterm-model/src/session_store.rs:2186 (flush_deferred_writes, no caller); session_store.rs:2201 (set_connection_status, no caller); session_store.rs:1134 (failed write marked dirty) |

The row (failed creation, lock failure) never goes away. The pane Dismiss button cited by the first auditor is unreachable in Electron. | app-rs/crates/treeterm-model/src/session_store.rs:1525 (dismiss_workspace, no caller); app-rs/crates/treeterm-app/src/tree_pane.rs:548 (Dismiss -> confirm_remove -> remove_workspace) |

The function exists, but nothing in app-rs calls it, not even tests, and set_connection_status is never called either. On Reconnecting/Disconnected AppStore disposes the old store straight away and the next generation rebuilds from disk. A body write that failed as the connection dropped is marked dirty (session_store.rs:1134, 'Always re-written by the next reconnect flush') and is then lost. This is the same failure class as the orphan-TTY ptyId:null bug. | app-rs/crates/treeterm-model/src/session_store.rs:2186 (flush_deferred_writes); app-rs/crates/treeterm-model/src/app_store.rs:235 (retire on status change) |

So mutations this app commits stay queued and are re-applied over every external body. Failure 1: with Record R1 and Ack R1 still pending, Rust records R2 (unread). The next edit by another client, carrying {R2, ack R1}, makes Rust re-apply the stale Record R1, which gives {R1,R1}. Rust's own unread marker is erased and the result is written to all clients. Failure 2: another client's new R2 marker is overwritten the same way. Each stale re-apply also causes an extra write. | app-rs/crates/treeterm-core/src/attention.rs:186 (reconcile_write, unused for own writes); app-rs/crates/treeterm-model/src/session_store.rs:709 (own echo returns early); app-rs/crates/treeterm-model/src/workspace_store.rs:1608, :1616 |

NotRepo changes nothing, and no model code updates is_git_repo or git_root_path after a workspace is created. A workspace that becomes a repo (git init) or stops being one keeps its old git state. The header git buttons (gated on is_git_repo, app_view.rs:1634) and the fetch and PR steps do not follow. In Rust's favour, a transient getInfo failure no longer flips the workspace to non-git. This gap is shared with the createSessionStore updateGitInfo chunk. | app-rs/crates/treeterm-model/src/git_controller.rs:47 (Deps::refresh_info); app-rs/crates/treeterm-model/src/workspace_store.rs:1530 (set_git_branch) |

If it cannot, the workspace has no git status button, no Pull button, no PR state, and no tree PR badge or behind count. Electron's controller does not depend on GitHub. This only happens when HTTP/TLS setup fails, so it is rare. | app-rs/crates/treeterm-model/src/session_store.rs:855 (new_workspace_store, if let Some(github)), :374 (github_client) |

So the tab opens localhost root where Electron shows nothing. Hosts that WHATWG normalises to an allowed one (127.1, 0x7f000001, %6Cocalhost, localhost\x) are refused in Rust. The first audit's claim that evil.com was reachable was wrong. | app-rs/crates/treeterm-model/src/apps.rs:210 |

Nothing writes settings.json recentDirectories outside tests. The port keeps per-connection recents in ui-state.json instead (tree_pane.rs:157), and that is what the Open Workspace dialog shows in both apps. Nothing reads settings.recentDirectories in either app, so only the file content differs. | app-rs/crates/treeterm-core/src/settings/mod.rs:115 (Settings::add_recent_directory, called only from tests) |

In the port both the counter and the TrafficMeter belong to one connection generation, so after a reconnect the tooltip total restarts at 0 and the connection badge reads the loading text until the first sample. The port also counts HTTP/2 framing, which README Status mentions ('counted at the daemon socket') but does not list as a deliberate difference. Same finding as c03-22/c03-23. | app-rs/crates/treeterm-core/src/daemon.rs:79 (new ByteCounter per DaemonConnection), app-rs/crates/treeterm-model/src/session_store.rs:402 (TrafficMeter per SessionStore) |

If the daemon never sends a result, the Rust loop waits for the client backstop (timeout + 10 s = 25 s), then shows ExecError::NoResult, whose text contains the whole sh -c script. In that case Electron leaves the run pending and keeps polling. | app-rs/crates/treeterm-model/src/system_monitor.rs:83 (poll loop timer) |

The port has no dispose. The model page is cached per Files tab by path and only hidden while a text or image file is shown. Going A -> text file -> A reuses the live page, so the camera orbit/zoom and the Wireframe toggle persist where Electron re-frames with wireframe off. The page also keeps the old bytes (c26-151). | app-rs/crates/treeterm-app/src/file_browser_view.rs:389 (page_for); app-rs/crates/treeterm-app/src/browser_view.rs:626 (set_shown) |
| [c53-60](chunks/c53-renderer_lib_modelLoader.md) | src/renderer/lib/modelStage.ts:118 | PARTIAL | Files/editor | On unmount, forceContextLoss releases the WebGL context and the canvas is removed | No explicit release. The model webview, with its WebGL context, stays alive and hidden while a text or image file is shown. It is dropped only when another PDF/model replaces it or the Files tab closes. Resource lifetime only. | app-rs/crates/treeterm-app/src/file_browser_view.rs:389 (page_for); app-rs/crates/treeterm-app/src/browser_view.rs:626 (set_shown) |
